      f\"Skipped unexpected disposition {disposition!r} for {disp['obligation_id']}\"\n                )\n                continue\n            tv.assert_tuple(\n                relation,\n                values,\n                grounding=disposition_groundings(disp),\n            )\n            clause_asserted += 1\n        elif relation == \"contract_lifecycle_status\":\n            if continue\n            tv.assert_tuple(\n                relation,\n                values,\n                grounding=disposition_groundings(disp),\n            )\n            lifecycle_asserted += 1\n\n    limitations.append(\n        f\"contract_clause_present: {clause_unresolved} UNRESOLVED obligation(s) \"\n        \"record no world tuple per kernel unresolved-state policy.\"\n    )\n    limitations.append(\n        f\"Persisted {clause_asserted} contract_clause_present and \"\n        f\"{lifecycle_asserted} contract_lifecycle_status WORLD tuples from \"\n        \"05_dispositions.json.\"\n    )\n    limitations.append(\n        \"Purpose-scoped dispositions (entity_identity_judgment, \"\n        \"invoice_counterparty_association,
